                  [32m'      // Case 1: Reordering within the same column\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      if (sourcePosition === targetPosition) {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        return // No change\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      }\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m'          SET position = position - 1 \n'[39m +

                  [32m'        `).run(sourceColumnId, sourcePosition, targetPosition)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      } else {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        // Shift cards between targetPosition and sourcePosition - 1 up by 1\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        db.prepare(`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'          UPDATE cards \n'[39m +
                  [32m'          SET position = position + 1 \n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +

                  [32m"      db.prepare('UPDATE cards SET position = ? WHERE id = ?').run(targetPosition, cardId)\n"[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    } else {\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      // Case 2: Moving to a different column\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      // 1. Shift cards in source column down (decrement positions after sourcePosition)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      db.prepare(`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        UPDATE cards \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        SET position = position - 1 \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        WHERE column_id = ? AND position > ?\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      `).run(sourceColumnId, sourcePosition)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      // 2. Shift cards in target column up (increment positions at or after targetPosition)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      db.prepare(`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        UPDATE cards \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        SET position = position + 1 \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        WHERE column_id = ? AND position >= ?\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      `).run(targetColumnId, targetPosition)\n'[39m +
                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m"      // 3. Update the moved card's column and position\n"[39m +
                  [32m'      db.prepare(`\n'[39m +
                  [32m'        UPDATE cards \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        SET column_id = ?, position = ? \n'[39m +
                  [32m'        WHERE id = ?\n'[39m +
                  [32m'      `).run(targetColumnId, targetPosition, cardId)\n'[39m +

                  [32m'\n'[39m +
                  [32m'    // Safety measure: Normalize positions in both columns to ensure 0-based, contiguous, no gaps, no duplicates\n'[39m +
